{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CgmzSnJPjRs1Gfrx4AndY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DXUgEsY7PEihHQLUbMo9rc","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/e9ff8fb461fe/workspace/target; python3 - <<'EOF'\np='specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md'\ns=open(p).read()\nanchor=\"\\n## 6. SessionEntry Objects\"\nnew='''\n### 5.9 session_list\n\nThe answer to a `list_sessions`: the conversations of the connection's authenticated user, as their store's `listFor` returns them. It is sent only when asked, never pushed, and is no turn, so no `stream_end` follows it.\n\n```json\n{\n  \"type\": \"session_list\",\n  \"sessions\": [\n    {\"sessionId\": \"s2\", \"createdAt\": \"2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z\", \"updatedAt\": \"2026-01-01T00:02:00.000Z\", \"entryCount\": 4}\n  ]\n}\n```\n\n| Field      | Type   | Description                                                                              |\n| ---------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |\n| `type`     | string | `\"session_list\"`                                                                         |\n| `sessions` | array  | `{sessionId, createdAt, updatedAt, entryCount}` per conversation, newest activity first |\n\n- The list is the user's the connection authenticated as, never one named in the frame, and it carries conversation metadata only, no entries; `createdAt` and `updatedAt` are ISO-8601 strings, and `limit` and `before` reach the store as the frame sent them ([validated by: answers list_sessions with the session user’s summaries, dates as ISO strings, and starts no stream](../../src/transport/ws/messageHandler.test.ts#L90)).\n'''\nassert anchor in s\ns=s.replace(anchor, new+anchor,1)\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\n\np='CHANGELOG.md'\ns=open(p).read()\nold=\"### Added\\n\\n\"\nentry=\"### Added\\n\\n- A client can ask for its own conversations over the WebSocket: send `{type: 'list_sessions', limit?, before?}` and the engine answers `{type: 'session_list', sessions}` from your store's `listFor`, with `createdAt` and `updatedAt` as ISO-8601 strings. The list is always the connected user's — a `userId` on the frame is ignored — and it is answered at once, even while a reply is streaming. To open one, reconnect with `?sessionId=<id>`. See `specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md` § 4.3 and § 5.9.\\n\"\ni=s.index(old)\ns=s[:i]+entry+s[i+len(\"### Added\\n\\n\")-1:] if False else s[:i]+entry+\"\\n\"+s[i+len(old):]\nopen(p,'w').write(s)\nEOF\nsed -n 9,14p CHANGELOG.md | cut -c1-120"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":969,"cache_read_input_tokens":77413,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":969},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"926ccd8c-95d3-4391-85f8-d038c835401a","uuid":"07be175a-237a-4546-9f1a-cf24f3050c92","timestamp":"2026-11-06T16:29:29.698Z","request_id":"req_011CgmzSn3WF2LHsGXjomybv"}

It is sent only when asked, never pushed, and is no turn, so no `stream_end` follows it.","+","+```json","+{","+  \"type\": \"session_list\",","+  \"sessions\": [","+    {\"sessionId\": \"s2\", \"createdAt\": \"2026-01-01T00:00:00.000Z\", \"updatedAt\": \"2026-01-01T00:02:00.000Z\", \"entryCount\": 4}","+  ]","+}","+```","+","+| Field      | Type   | Description                                                                              |","+| ---------- | ------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |","+| `type`     | string | `\"session_list\"`                                                                         |","+| `sessions` | array  | `{sessionId, createdAt, updatedAt, entryCount}` per conversation, newest activity first |","+","+- The list is the user's the connection authenticated as, never one named in the frame, and it carries conversation metadata only, no entries; `createdAt` and `updatedAt` are ISO-8601 strings, and `limit` and `before` reach the store as the frame sent them ([validated by: answers list_sessions with the session user’s summaries, dates as ISO strings, and starts no stream](../../src/transport/ws/messageHandler.test.ts#L90)).","+"," ## 6. SessionEntry Objects"," "," A SessionEntry represents a single piece of content in the conversation. Every entry has a `role` field that determines its shape. The `role` acts as a discriminant for the union type."]}]},{"filePath":"/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/e9ff8fb461fe/workspace/target/CHANGELOG.md","hunks":[{"oldStart":10,"oldLines":6,"newStart":10,"newLines":8,"lines":[" "," ### Added"," ","+- A client can ask for its own conversations over the WebSocket: send `{type: 'list_sessions', limit?, before?}` and the engine answers `{type: 'session_list', sessions}` from your store's `listFor`, with `createdAt` and `updatedAt` as ISO-8601 strings. The list is always the connected user's — a `userId` on the frame is ignored — and it is answered at once, even while a reply is streaming. To open one, reconnect with `?sessionId=<id>`. See `specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md` § 4.3 and § 5.9.","+"," - `SessionStore.listFor(userId, options?)`, an optional store member that answers the summaries of a user's conversations — `sessionId`, `createdAt`, `updatedAt` and `entryCount` — most recent activity first, so a consumer can build a conversation list instead of only rejoining one. Nothing in the engine calls it yet: the store answers the list, and the route or frame that carries it to a client is still yours to write. `InMemorySessionStore` and `MongoSessionStore` both implement it; a store of your own may leave it out. A summary never carries a conversation's entries or its credentials. Page with `limit` (50 by default, 200 at most, and `0` answers nothing) and `before`, which takes the last summary you saw: the cursor carries both `updatedAt` and `sessionId`, so two conversations saved in the same millisecond cannot straddle a page boundary and go unlisted. See `docs/session-stores.md` § Listing a user's conversations."," "," ### Changed"]}]}],"moreFiles":0,"changedFiles":["/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/e9ff8fb461fe/workspace/target/specs/hal-engine-websocket-protocol/spec.md","/var/tmp/loop-bench/runs/e9ff8fb461fe/workspace/target/CHANGELOG.md"]}},"tool_result_meta":[{"id":"toolu_01DXUgEsY7PEihHQLUbMo9rc","permission_decision":{"decision":"accept","source":"config","reason_type":"mode"}}]}
